- candidate chunk-c7fe9475868cd154a1c7e73956ed4139 doc=doc-04ef7ad9e1765bcf95cbd804c813863b sections=[Ultimate Voltage (UV) > Infrastructure Improvements > Component Assembly Line] text="Component Assembly Line\nThe Component Assembly Line (CoAL) is a new multiblock for assembling components, such as Electric Motors, Robot Arms, and Sensors. It provides a 25% discount on both resources and time, as well as skipping the Assembly Line , two incredible advantages that mean it should be the only source of components whenever it can be used. The main cost of the CoAL is the 43 tiered Component Assembly Line Casings that determine the maximum available tier of components: a full CoAL takes 688 Electric Motors, 430 Electric Pistons, and 344 Robot Arms of its tier. In UV, both the UV and ZPM CoAL tiers are viable. The ZPM CoAL doesn't help with UV components, but it is much cheaper than the UV CoAL, and still useful for LuV and ZPM superconductors in particular, which are used for Fusion Reactor coils and Wetware Mainframes. The UV CoAL adds the ability to make UV components, but is very expensive, particularly on Americium, until late in the tier."
- candidate chunk-686e26db985b551ddf9bfe9947a3526c doc=doc-2dca4aebd2d58cec651996f808e3aec7 sections=[Tier] text="GTNH is divided up into tiers. Each new tier unlocks more materials, machines, multiblocks and mods available to use. The first ten tiers up to UHV are considered fully implemented, while major changes are still in development for UEV-MAX. Tier is a common term used by many mods, to represent the player's level of progress of any sort; other tiered mechanics are listed below.\nBlood Magic Altar • Coils • Electricity • Circuits • IC2 Crops • Rockets • Thaumcraft Wands\nTier | EU [ 1 ] | Casing [ 2 ] | Materials | Coil | Glass | Dimensions\n0 Stone | Wood, Stone, Flint, Leather, Coal, Charcoal, (fuel) Coke, Copper, Tin, Lapis, Redstone, Gold, Ender Pearl, Slimeball, Sticky Resin, Glue, Iron, Wrought Iron , Glass, Bronze, Lava, Water, Soularium\n1 Steam | Bronze | Nether Quartz , Sulfur, Rubber, Red Alloy , Obzinite, Obsidian [ 3 ] , Diamond, Lead [ 4 ] , Bismuth [ 5 ] , Steel | Nether [ 6 ]\n2 LV | 32 | Steel | Oil, Seed Oil, Polyethylene, Light Fuel, Refined Glue, Advanced Glue, Hydrogen, Oxygen, Helium, Nitrogen, Lithium, Sodium, Annealed Copper, Magnalium, Cupronickel, Silver [ 7 ] , Nickel [ 7 ] , Electrum, Glowstone [ 8 ] , Zinc, Cryolite, Chlorine, Damascus Steel, Vanadium, Vanadiumsteel, Molybdenum, Vis Shards, metal blocks | Cupronickel | Twilight Forest\n3 MV | 128 | Aluminium | Redstone Alloy, Cobalt, Ardite, Thaumium, Void Metal, Manasteel, Energetic Alloy, Pulsating Iron, Dark Steel, Electrical Steel, Soularium, Chrome, Silicon Solar Grade , Kanthal, IC2 Coolant, Diesel, Benzene, Stainless Steel | Kanthal\n4 HV | 512 | Stainless Steel | Vibrant Alloy, Eye of Ender [ 9 ] , Neodymium, PTFE, Radium, Radon, Rutile, Shadow Metal, Titanium | Nichrome | Glass (vanilla) | Rocket T1 Pers. Dimension Last Millenium The End [ 6 ]\n5 EV | 2k | Titanium | Tungsten, Tungstensteel, Radon, End Steel, Crystalline Alloy, Melodic Alloy, Enderium, TPV-Alloy, HSS-G, Platinum, Iridium, Terrasteel | TPV-Alloy | Managlass Reinforced Glass TRB Glass Block [ 10 ] | Rocket T2, Spectre\n6 IV | 8k | Tungstensteel | Naquadah, Stellar Alloy, Osmium, HSS-S, Elementium, Gaia Spirit, Rhodium, Palladium | HSS-G HSS-S | Alfglass TsRB Glass Block [ 11 ] Thorium Yttrium Glass Block | Rocket T3\n7 LuV | 32k | Rhodium-Plated Palladium | Europium | Naquadah Naquadah Alloy | RPPRB Glass Block [ 12 ]"
- candidate chunk-de397ab18f8ce9d13b603dd8ccf0ee0b doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets > Questbook] text="Questbook\nThe Better Questing quest tab for rockets, \"Space Race\", unlocks upon completing the IV Circuits and HV Assembling Machine quests in the HV quest tab. It contains the progression for rocket tiers, space survival systems, and rocket fuels, starting with a quest to craft the NASA Workbench."
- candidate chunk-33ead29e8cd8bacf04be05f96c988afe doc=doc-8666c624815c422fd88b0cdfb5cbfb2a sections=[High Voltage (HV) > Progression > Preparation for making the rocket] text="Preparation for making the rocket\nYou need a NASA workbench and a Implosion Compressor for your first rocket. You will find that the NASA Workbench is a particularly arduous craft that for some reason requires you to use GT tools in a crafting grid in HV. On the other hand, the Implosion Compressor should be trivial for the average player, regardless of setup.\nThe amount of explosives needed for use with the Implosion Compressor is non-trivial, though. You will be expending several stacks of TNT. If your energy setup is based on benzene, you will find that obtaining enough toluene for TNT will be trivial if you upgrade your setup to use a Distillation Tower instead of single-block distilleries. However, if your energy setup is based on oil, you will need some additional processing steps-- diverting some Heavy Fuel for use in a distillery or Distillation Tower will likely be the best way.\nYou'll need 37 T1 heavy-duty alloy plates and a ton of additional materials. Check the quest book. You'll need a T1 rocket, an oxygen equipment set with 2 light oxygen tanks (only 1 is very risky for resource collecting), an oxygen collector and compressor, a fuel loader, and 9 landing pads."
- candidate chunk-5ff54a6f190364d05242ad8bcac58ed8 doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets > Crafting Tier 2+ Rockets] text="Crafting Tier 2+ Rockets\nEntrance to a Moon Dungeon\nThe NASA Workbench requires new schematics to craft higher tiers of rockets. These schematics are found in dungeons on planets in tier order. Tier 2 rocket schematics, for example, can be found in the reward room of Moon Dungeons. Dungeon entrances are deep holes on the surface that have unnatural brick blocks on the bottom.\nThe dungeons are naturally generated and so may be difficult to find. It is important to mark the location of each dungeon found as the coordinates are the same across each planet.\nHigher tiers of rockets require different kinds of Rocket Fuels ."
- candidate chunk-697978597417732685928c2725346ae9 doc=doc-8bdfdac1b12c28b48cfd59b3c35795d1 sections=[Highly Ultimate Voltage (UHV) > Infrastructure Improvements > Component Assembly Line] text="Component Assembly Line\nA UV Tier Component Assembly Line\nThe UHV Component Assembly Line (CoAL) is very useful due to how often UHV components are used, particularly for UHV superconductors in Bio Mainframes, Compact Fusion Reactor Mk4s, and new multiblocks like the Integrated Ore Factory. Its extremely high Cosmic Neutronium cost makes it very difficult to afford prior to the Mk2 Space Elevator Mining Module, although sufficient investment into Meteors can also be used. Players who have not made any Component Assembly Line yet should make one in UHV."
- candidate chunk-2a370709a809e53184319ca5ef0fd2a4 doc=doc-a509849f031bb1dadb346a8565bf75e8 sections=[Mega Ultimate Voltage (UMV) > Progression > Component Assembly Line] text="Component Assembly Line\nThe UMV Component Assembly Line (CoAL) is one of the most useful CoAL tiers, and should be made very early in UMV. It is fairly cheap with an Eternal DTPF for SpaceTime and Hypogen, and provides a discount on the large amount of UMV components needed for Wireless Energy Hatches and the Eye of Harmony. It also covers any skipped lower tier CoALs, and doubles the speed of all lower tier components."
- candidate chunk-fc19ae52afa2b36372260d4e67bf17e2 doc=doc-d8847ae5c01144e82bc225fe54375054 sections=[Zero Point Module (ZPM) > Progression > Tier 6 Rocket] text="Tier 6 Rocket\nThe Tier 6 Rocket can be crafted once a Mk2 Fusion Reactor has been used to produce Tritanium, which is needed for the Tier 6 Heavy Duty Plate. There are two planets available, Proteus and Triton, both moons of Neptune. They contain a few new ores, most notably Neutronium, Black Plutonium, Plutonium 241, Uranium 235, Niobium, and Yttrium, which can be harvested either with conventional Ore Drilling Plants or with Void Miners . Deuterium and Ethylene are available as underground fluids from a Fluid Drilling Rig . The Tier 6 Rocket also unlocks the ability to make Planetary Gas Siphons above Neptune for Tritium, Helium-3, Ammonia, and Xenon."
- evidence chunk-a88392d5df0ac358887fd2e3c27c3f6e doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets > Crafting a Tier 1 Rocket > NASA Workbench] text="NASA Workbench\nThe NASA Workbench is used to make rockets and other Galacticraft vehicles. Notable crafting components used to make the NASA Workbench include an HV Assembling Machine, 2 IV Circuits, 3 EV Circuits, Energetic Alloy frame boxes, and 6 Nether Star Plates (requiring 9 Nether Stars for a Block of Nether Star)."
- evidence chunk-dd32ee9a7437c9bdc478d307b750b39f doc=doc-04ef7ad9e1765bcf95cbd804c813863b sections=[Ultimate Voltage (UV) > Progression > Tier 7 Rocket] text="Tier 7 Rocket\nThe Tier 7 Rocket is available as soon as the player is able to power their Assembly Line with two UV Energy Hatches to overclock it to UHV. It unlocks four new planets: Haumea, Makemake, Pluto, and the Kuiper Belt, which should only be mined using Void Miners at this point. The most useful planet to mine is Haumea, which has the earliest source of Nether Star Ore as well as a high chance for Infused Gold Ore, making it very useful for setting up the second tier of Naquadah Fuel. Other new ores are Borax, Red Garnet, Yellow Garnet, and Chrysotile. The available underground fluids are Hydrofluoric Acid on Makemake, and Oxygen, Nitrogen, Fluorine, and Liquid Air on Pluto.\nThe dungeons containing the next tier of schematic are found on Pluto, alongside the Draconic Core Schematic for the Draconic Evolution Fusion Crafter . It is highly recommended to place the Tier 8 Rocket Schematic in a singleblock Scanner (a Research Station in Scanner mode will not work) and duplicate it using a UV Circuit Assembler, because two Tier 8 Rocket Schematics will be needed: one for making the Tier 8 Rocket, and one to be researched for the Shuttle Schematic for the Mothership ."

In UV, both the UV and ZPM CoAL tiers are viable. The ZPM CoAL doesn't help with UV components, but it is much cheaper than the UV CoAL, and still useful for LuV and ZPM superconductors in particular, which are used for Fusion Reactor coils and Wetware Mainframes. The UV CoAL adds the ability to make UV components, but is very expensive, particularly on Americium, until late in the tier."

## Turn 9 — What does the Galacticraft Fuel Loader need, and how much fuel can a T1 rocket hold?

Expected goal rubric: Identify Fuel Loader requirements and T1 rocket fuel capacity.

### Lane 1 — memory=false

Settings: `{Mode:grounded Strategy:structural TaskMemory:false Rewrite:false}`

Answer status: answer
Refusal: false
Answer: The Galacticraft Fuel Loader requires 91 EU/s to run. Once a rocket is placed on the launch pad, the Fuel Loader will pipe fuel from its tank into the rocket; it can be filled with Galacticraft Liquid Canisters, fluid cells, or piped in through a tank. A Tier 1 Rocket holds up to 5 Buckets of fuel.

- candidate chunk-576f35aa4c7d75f033ca70a4306494dd doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets > Space Travel > Building Launchpad] text="Building Launchpad\nThe Rocket requires rocket fuel and a launch pad to launch.\n- A launch pad is formed by placing a 3x3 square on the ground of Galacticraft Launch Pads. Right click to place a rocket on the launch pad.\n- A Galacticraft Fuel Loader can be placed next to the launch pad. It requires 91 EU/s to run. Once a rocket is placed on the launch pad, the Fuel Loader will pipe fuel from its tank into the rocket. A Fuel Loader can be filled with Galacticraft Liquid Canisters, fluid cells or piped in through a tank. All tiers of rocket hold enough fuel capacity for at least one round trip. The Tier 1 Rocket holds up to 5 Buckets of fuel."
- candidate chunk-9789157d3151f95d6483f88ec29e24bb doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets] text="A Tier 1 Rocket, on launch pad\nThe Rocket is a vehicle added by the Galacticraft mod that enables travel to different planets . These planets often contain ore veins and underground fluid veins which are inaccessible in other dimensions and are required for Tier progression."

It is highly recommended to place the Tier 8 Rocket Schematic in a singleblock Scanner (a Research Station in Scanner mode will not work) and duplicate it using a UV Circuit Assembler, because two Tier 8 Rocket Schematics will be needed: one for making the Tier 8 Rocket, and one to be researched for the Shuttle Schematic for the Mothership ."
- candidate chunk-bd30c29740c8b96a881cb63d33fa8578 doc=doc-0acf46d9067c834f0c19087002ed00b0 sections=[Insane Voltage (IV) > Progression Goals > Rockets] text="Rockets\nUnlike most tiers, IV contains two different tiers of rockets and associated sets of planets that they unlock. The Tier 3 Rocket is available early in the tier, only being gated behind the IV Assembling Machine to make some of the parts. The Tier 4 Rocket, on the other hand, requires the Assembly Line, meaning it is unavailable until just before the start of LuV. In addition, both tiers require an upgraded rocket fuel, Dense Hydrazine, in order to use, which will require building a Chemical Plant for players who opted to use Cetane-Boosted Diesel for the first two tiers. Most planets from Tier 3 onwards also require an upgraded set of Thermal Padding, which needs PBI to make, and the Tier 4 planet Venus requires a full set of armor with the SpaceSuit property. For a full list of resources available on the Tier 3 and 4 planets, see the Planets page. The Tier 3 rocket also unlocks the ability to make a space station over Jupiter, although the Tier 4 rocket is required to make the Planetary Gas Siphon that makes such a space station useful."
- candidate chunk-71277135f5725a920c81ac04ea30ed9f doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets > Trivia] text="Trivia\n- In 2.1.1.0 additional rocket fuels were added that could be made in the Large Chemical Reactor as an alternative to the rocket fuels made in the GT++ Chemical Plant . In 2.2.0.0 their recipes were removed, but could still be used as rocket fuels. As of 2.6.1, they no longer appear in NEI and likely have been removed.\nDeprecated Fuel | Max. Rocket\nDeprecated Fuel: LMP-103S | Max. Rocket: T4\nDeprecated Fuel: Monomethlyhydrazine Fuel Mix | Max. Rocket: T6\nDeprecated Fuel: Unsymmetrical Dimethylhydrazine Fuel Mix | Max. Rocket: T8"
- candidate chunk-a5f28cb4868a57c9395131f83cfe3cd6 doc=doc-d38e0fb2c49edead8e172a0081f57d61 sections=[Extreme Voltage (EV) > Progression > Your second rocket / Late-EV] text="Your second rocket / Late-EV\nAfter getting the EV field generator you needed for the rocket computer, it's time to build your second rocket. It's a pretty straightforward step. If you forgot or didn't get much meteoric iron, you have to get back to the moon and mine some. I recommend you to have 12 stacks or 45 stacks if you don't want to go back to the moon again for meteoric iron (also for t3 rocket). With the exception of several new parts, which are booster and t2 landing module, the rest goes the same as t1 rocket but is more expensive.\nYou only need a trip to mars for you to get all the resources needed. Also, T2 lander doesn't need you to slow down when approaching as it's a balloon that bounces after it reaches the surface. Your mission there is to collect desh vein (it also has tungsten ore), tungsten vein (also provides lithium), and pitchblende vein (also provides thorium) if you didn't get enough uranium by centrifuging thorium ore. Remember to explore mars more for finding the mars dungeon which contains the T3 rocket blueprint."
- candidate chunk-7b3080f71e27c30f49c03ac0d082f953 doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets > Crafting a Tier 1 Rocket > Implosion Compressor] text="Implosion Compressor\nThe Implosion Compressor is required to craft Galacticraft compressed metal plates, which are used in rockets and oxygen systems."
- candidate chunk-8c0401ed3a60ab21dc1e43801523f528 doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets > Crafting a Tier 1 Rocket > Storage Rockets] text="Storage Rockets\nIn the T1 Rocket craft, there is an optional slot below the rocket control computer to add a copper, iron, or gold chest into (these recipes are also available in NEI ). This will add storage space to the rocket, but it will not be detected by the T1 Rocket quest in the Space Race tab. These can be converted to the normal rocket in a crafting grid."

Rocket: T6 | Infrastructure Requirements: Chemical Plant HV/Tungstensteel\nFuel: H8N4C2O4 | Max. Rocket: T9 | Infrastructure Requirements: Chemical Plant HV/Laurenium"
- candidate chunk-33ead29e8cd8bacf04be05f96c988afe doc=doc-8666c624815c422fd88b0cdfb5cbfb2a sections=[High Voltage (HV) > Progression > Preparation for making the rocket] text="Preparation for making the rocket\nYou need a NASA workbench and a Implosion Compressor for your first rocket. You will find that the NASA Workbench is a particularly arduous craft that for some reason requires you to use GT tools in a crafting grid in HV. On the other hand, the Implosion Compressor should be trivial for the average player, regardless of setup.\nThe amount of explosives needed for use with the Implosion Compressor is non-trivial, though. You will be expending several stacks of TNT. If your energy setup is based on benzene, you will find that obtaining enough toluene for TNT will be trivial if you upgrade your setup to use a Distillation Tower instead of single-block distilleries. However, if your energy setup is based on oil, you will need some additional processing steps-- diverting some Heavy Fuel for use in a distillery or Distillation Tower will likely be the best way.\nYou'll need 37 T1 heavy-duty alloy plates and a ton of additional materials. Check the quest book. You'll need a T1 rocket, an oxygen equipment set with 2 light oxygen tanks (only 1 is very risky for resource collecting), an oxygen collector and compressor, a fuel loader, and 9 landing pads."
- candidate chunk-dd32ee9a7437c9bdc478d307b750b39f doc=doc-04ef7ad9e1765bcf95cbd804c813863b sections=[Ultimate Voltage (UV) > Progression > Tier 7 Rocket] text="Tier 7 Rocket\nThe Tier 7 Rocket is available as soon as the player is able to power their Assembly Line with two UV Energy Hatches to overclock it to UHV. It unlocks four new planets: Haumea, Makemake, Pluto, and the Kuiper Belt, which should only be mined using Void Miners at this point. The most useful planet to mine is Haumea, which has the earliest source of Nether Star Ore as well as a high chance for Infused Gold Ore, making it very useful for setting up the second tier of Naquadah Fuel. Other new ores are Borax, Red Garnet, Yellow Garnet, and Chrysotile. The available underground fluids are Hydrofluoric Acid on Makemake, and Oxygen, Nitrogen, Fluorine, and Liquid Air on Pluto.\nThe dungeons containing the next tier of schematic are found on Pluto, alongside the Draconic Core Schematic for the Draconic Evolution Fusion Crafter . It is highly recommended to place the Tier 8 Rocket Schematic in a singleblock Scanner (a Research Station in Scanner mode will not work) and duplicate it using a UV Circuit Assembler, because two Tier 8 Rocket Schematics will be needed: one for making the Tier 8 Rocket, and one to be researched for the Shuttle Schematic for the Mothership ."
- candidate chunk-7b3080f71e27c30f49c03ac0d082f953 doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets > Crafting a Tier 1 Rocket > Implosion Compressor] text="Implosion Compressor\nThe Implosion Compressor is required to craft Galacticraft compressed metal plates, which are used in rockets and oxygen systems."
- candidate chunk-a5f28cb4868a57c9395131f83cfe3cd6 doc=doc-d38e0fb2c49edead8e172a0081f57d61 sections=[Extreme Voltage (EV) > Progression > Your second rocket / Late-EV] text="Your second rocket / Late-EV\nAfter getting the EV field generator you needed for the rocket computer, it's time to build your second rocket. It's a pretty straightforward step. If you forgot or didn't get much meteoric iron, you have to get back to the moon and mine some. I recommend you to have 12 stacks or 45 stacks if you don't want to go back to the moon again for meteoric iron (also for t3 rocket). With the exception of several new parts, which are booster and t2 landing module, the rest goes the same as t1 rocket but is more expensive.\nYou only need a trip to mars for you to get all the resources needed. Also, T2 lander doesn't need you to slow down when approaching as it's a balloon that bounces after it reaches the surface. Your mission there is to collect desh vein (it also has tungsten ore), tungsten vein (also provides lithium), and pitchblende vein (also provides thorium) if you didn't get enough uranium by centrifuging thorium ore. Remember to explore mars more for finding the mars dungeon which contains the T3 rocket blueprint."
- candidate chunk-bd30c29740c8b96a881cb63d33fa8578 doc=doc-0acf46d9067c834f0c19087002ed00b0 sections=[Insane Voltage (IV) > Progression Goals > Rockets] text="Rockets\nUnlike most tiers, IV contains two different tiers of rockets and associated sets of planets that they unlock. The Tier 3 Rocket is available early in the tier, only being gated behind the IV Assembling Machine to make some of the parts. The Tier 4 Rocket, on the other hand, requires the Assembly Line, meaning it is unavailable until just before the start of LuV. In addition, both tiers require an upgraded rocket fuel, Dense Hydrazine, in order to use, which will require building a Chemical Plant for players who opted to use Cetane-Boosted Diesel for the first two tiers. Most planets from Tier 3 onwards also require an upgraded set of Thermal Padding, which needs PBI to make, and the Tier 4 planet Venus requires a full set of armor with the SpaceSuit property. For a full list of resources available on the Tier 3 and 4 planets, see the Planets page. The Tier 3 rocket also unlocks the ability to make a space station over Jupiter, although the Tier 4 rocket is required to make the Planetary Gas Siphon that makes such a space station useful."
- candidate chunk-71277135f5725a920c81ac04ea30ed9f doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets > Trivia] text="Trivia\n- In 2.1.1.0 additional rocket fuels were added that could be made in the Large Chemical Reactor as an alternative to the rocket fuels made in the GT++ Chemical Plant . In 2.2.0.0 their recipes were removed, but could still be used as rocket fuels. As of 2.6.1, they no longer appear in NEI and likely have been removed.\nDeprecated Fuel | Max. Rocket\nDeprecated Fuel: LMP-103S | Max. Rocket: T4\nDeprecated Fuel: Monomethlyhydrazine Fuel Mix | Max. Rocket: T6\nDeprecated Fuel: Unsymmetrical Dimethylhydrazine Fuel Mix | Max. Rocket: T8"
- candidate chunk-8c0401ed3a60ab21dc1e43801523f528 doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets > Crafting a Tier 1 Rocket > Storage Rockets] text="Storage Rockets\nIn the T1 Rocket craft, there is an optional slot below the rocket control computer to add a copper, iron, or gold chest into (these recipes are also available in NEI ). This will add storage space to the rocket, but it will not be detected by the T1 Rocket quest in the Space Race tab. These can be converted to the normal rocket in a crafting grid."
- evidence chunk-576f35aa4c7d75f033ca70a4306494dd doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets > Space Travel > Building Launchpad] text="Building Launchpad\nThe Rocket requires rocket fuel and a launch pad to launch.\n- A launch pad is formed by placing a 3x3 square on the ground of Galacticraft Launch Pads. Right click to place a rocket on the launch pad.\n- A Galacticraft Fuel Loader can be placed next to the launch pad. It requires 91 EU/s to run. Once a rocket is placed on the launch pad, the Fuel Loader will pipe fuel from its tank into the rocket. A Fuel Loader can be filled with Galacticraft Liquid Canisters, fluid cells or piped in through a tank. All tiers of rocket hold enough fuel capacity for at least one round trip. The Tier 1 Rocket holds up to 5 Buckets of fuel."
- evidence chunk-9789157d3151f95d6483f88ec29e24bb doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets] text="A Tier 1 Rocket, on launch pad\nThe Rocket is a vehicle added by the Galacticraft mod that enables travel to different planets . These planets often contain ore veins and underground fluid veins which are inaccessible in other dimensions and are required for Tier progression."

- candidate chunk-d76a162f5e2260296cff7570635391d4 doc=doc-0acf46d9067c834f0c19087002ed00b0 sections=[Insane Voltage (IV)] text="Insane Voltage (IV) is the fifth electric tier in GregTech: New Horizons. In this tier, multiple new multiblocks and mechanics are introduced, such as the Assembly Line , the Precise Assembler , and many more. This is also the tier where many parts of magic (Thaumcraft, etc.) are unlocked for players to use; however, as always, watch your warp effects if you have been getting warp. While transitioning from the old tier to a new tier is usually the hard part, the IV tier is by far the hardest tier to get through. If you managed to get through EV tier, prepare yourself because this tier requires a very heavy transition in almost every aspect of your base, including infrastructure and crafting. At this point, manual crafting is no longer a feasible option. With this being said, be prepared for a big change to your base!"
- candidate chunk-e53a7f7cd08cccca5de892a38320bc77 doc=doc-8666c624815c422fd88b0cdfb5cbfb2a sections=[High Voltage (HV)] text="High Voltage (HV) is the third electric tier in GregTech: New Horizons. In this tier, several new mechanics are introduced to you such as cleanrooms , space travel , and multiblock versions of your singleblock machines. With the introduction of the Cleanroom, your ability to produce more complex circuits and some of their critical components will be restricted to the inside of the Cleanroom multiblock structure, or other multiblocks that are also considered cleanrooms. Many important materials, such as titanium, can only be extracted from ores that exist on other planets and celestial bodies. You will find that all of these mechanics will carry on with you for the foreseeable future.\nUpon reaching HV, you should either have the ability to produce large quantities of stainless steel or quickly plan on developing the infrastructure to do so, as this is the first tier that starts demanding large quantities of its respective machine hull material, whether it's through the numerous single block machines or new multi-block machines such as the expensive but rewarding Distillation Tower."
- candidate chunk-9789157d3151f95d6483f88ec29e24bb doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets] text="A Tier 1 Rocket, on launch pad\nThe Rocket is a vehicle added by the Galacticraft mod that enables travel to different planets . These planets often contain ore veins and underground fluid veins which are inaccessible in other dimensions and are required for Tier progression."
- candidate chunk-8020be5e3374a08380c78b2c3d21be17 doc=doc-626c925b618145e4e43655f7673b36b4 sections=[Low Voltage (LV)] text="Low Voltage (LV) is the first electric tier in GregTech: New Horizons. With access to electricity comes new hazards and mechanics, but also far more variety of specialized machines that can process materials into new parts with greater efficiency."
- candidate chunk-223f6ac44ad47f65c142ca0045b909d0 doc=doc-4f4c543e0ece55aaf03e88c75a851b04 sections=[Extended Mega Ultimate Voltage (UXV)] text="Welcome to Extended Mega Ultimate Voltage (UXV) the thirteenth and final tier in GregTech: New Horizons. This tier begins with the production of Magnetohydrodynamically Constrained Star Matter (MHDCSM) and ends with a Stargate . In UXV, expect to spam even more machines and drastically increase power production to support laser energy hatches."
- candidate chunk-15e023bdae2263e4d4d830d8dfa03301 doc=doc-8bdfdac1b12c28b48cfd59b3c35795d1 sections=[Highly Ultimate Voltage (UHV)] text="Highly Ultimate Voltage (UHV) is the ninth electric tier in GregTech: New Horizons. It unlocks Bioprocessors, infinity ingots, and Draconic Evolution. The overall goal is the construction of the Mothership and Shuttle rocket to reach the Amun-Ra system, which is required to get your foot through the door of UEV . In UHV, many previously optional processing chains and multiblocks become necessary for progression."
- candidate chunk-d12a6c2242d53132071155189fe82899 doc=doc-4f4c543e0ece55aaf03e88c75a851b04 sections=[Extended Mega Ultimate Voltage (UXV) > Tips & Tricks > Extended Mega Ultimate Voltage (UXV) > Extended Mega Ultimate Voltage (UXV) > Extended Mega Ultimate Voltage (UXV) > Graviton Shards] text="Graviton Shards\nThe Stargate requires 512 Graviton Shards, which can be ejected from the Godforge after completing its final upgrade, END. After unlocking Inversion by reaching level 7 on each milestone, it becomes possible to scale the milestones infinitely to produce enough shards. Each of the four milestones can be pushed in order to rapidly gain shards, although it is more efficient to prioritize one or two milestones due to the fact that the number of shards gained scales quadratically, while the requirements only scale linearly. Charge can be pushed by using the unlimited maximum voltage unlocked by the TBF upgrade, Conversion by running a cheap recipe such as steel and re-macerating it repeatedly, Catalyst by using MHDCSM with a high fuel factor, and Composition by placing more Heliofusion Exoticizers in Magmatter mode."
- candidate chunk-c4f515f28759c9259bd668bf7140e214 doc=doc-a9f4f7813df646e0e5f0ab08ab29b50e sections=[Ludicrous Voltage (LuV)] text="Ludicrous Voltage (LuV) is the sixth electric tier in GregTech: New Horizons. This tier is largely centered around building the infrastructure necessary to construct the first tier of Fusion Reactor , as well as the new items and machines gated by fusion. This is the first tier where the Assembly Line is used to craft on-tier components as well as highly advanced machines. In addition, the costs and crafting trees for machines are even larger than in previous tiers, making fully automated on-demand crafting with AE2 absolutely necessary. The massive demand for robust infrastructure continues for the rest of the game; arrays of Volcanus consuming on-tier voltage are highly suggested."
- candidate chunk-05d33b53def3fa0645ccb3b4e9e5ffcb doc=doc-04ef7ad9e1765bcf95cbd804c813863b sections=[Ultimate Voltage (UV)] text="Ultimate Voltage (UV) is the eighth electric tier in GregTech: New Horizons. It is largely centered around the new Wetware circuit line, which introduces new multiblocks like the Quantum Computer and Research Station , as well a-s the new technologies that are unlocked with them, like the third tier of Fusion Reactor . It also introduces many multiblocks for improving infrastructure, including the Mega Alloy Blast Smelter , Component Assembly Line , and Space Elevator , as well as key automation tools like the Stocking Input Hatch."
- candidate chunk-fd0e7d7c4b95cf731b2074dbbab5e234 doc=doc-16afb764b518d80604353589d360da21 sections=[Medium Voltage (MV)] text="Medium Voltage (MV) is the second electric tier in GregTech: New Horizons. It introduces fewer new mechanics, but instead improves and expands what the player has already seen in LV ."

### Lane 2 — memory=true

Answer status: answer
Refusal: false
Answer: With your goal set as the first Moon trip and your current tier corrected to HV, the supplied evidence supports the following HV-stage preparation and launch steps:

1. Build the required infrastructure: you need a NASA Workbench and an Implosion Compressor for your first rocket. The NASA Workbench is a particularly arduous craft that requires using GT tools in a crafting grid in HV; the Implosion Compressor should be trivial regardless of setup.
2. Prepare explosives for the Implosion Compressor: this is non-trivial, as you will expend several stacks of TNT. If your energy setup is based on benzene, obtaining enough toluene for TNT is trivial if you upgrade to a Distillation Tower instead of single-block distilleries. If your energy setup is based on oil, you will need additional processing steps, likely diverting some Heavy Fuel for use in a distillery or Distillation Tower.
3. Gather materials: you will need 37 T1 heavy-duty alloy plates and a ton of additional materials; check the quest book. You will also need a T1 rocket, an oxygen equipment set with 2 light oxygen tanks (only 1 is very risky for resource collecting), an oxygen collector and compressor, a fuel loader, and 9 landing pads.
4. Build the launch pad: form a 3x3 square on the ground of Galacticraft Launch Pads, then right click to place the rocket on the launch pad.
5. Fuel the rocket: place a Galacticraft Fuel Loader next to the launch pad. It requires 91 EU/s to run. Once the rocket is placed on the launch pad, the Fuel Loader will pipe fuel from its tank into the rocket. The Fuel Loader can be filled with Galacticraft Liquid Canisters, fluid cells, or piped in through a tank. The Tier 1 Rocket holds up to 5 Buckets of fuel, and all tiers of rocket hold enough fuel capacity for at least one round trip.

Note: the evidence does not specify how to power the Fuel Loader from your steam power setup, nor does it provide the full recipe details for the NASA Workbench or T1 rocket components beyond the listed materials.
Goal state: "Plan the first Moon trip in GT:NH" (quote "Keep the first Moon trip as my goal.")

- candidate chunk-bd30c29740c8b96a881cb63d33fa8578 doc=doc-0acf46d9067c834f0c19087002ed00b0 sections=[Insane Voltage (IV) > Progression Goals > Rockets] text="Rockets\nUnlike most tiers, IV contains two different tiers of rockets and associated sets of planets that they unlock. The Tier 3 Rocket is available early in the tier, only being gated behind the IV Assembling Machine to make some of the parts. The Tier 4 Rocket, on the other hand, requires the Assembly Line, meaning it is unavailable until just before the start of LuV. In addition, both tiers require an upgraded rocket fuel, Dense Hydrazine, in order to use, which will require building a Chemical Plant for players who opted to use Cetane-Boosted Diesel for the first two tiers. Most planets from Tier 3 onwards also require an upgraded set of Thermal Padding, which needs PBI to make, and the Tier 4 planet Venus requires a full set of armor with the SpaceSuit property. For a full list of resources available on the Tier 3 and 4 planets, see the Planets page. The Tier 3 rocket also unlocks the ability to make a space station over Jupiter, although the Tier 4 rocket is required to make the Planetary Gas Siphon that makes such a space station useful."
- candidate chunk-fc19ae52afa2b36372260d4e67bf17e2 doc=doc-d8847ae5c01144e82bc225fe54375054 sections=[Zero Point Module (ZPM) > Progression > Tier 6 Rocket] text="Tier 6 Rocket\nThe Tier 6 Rocket can be crafted once a Mk2 Fusion Reactor has been used to produce Tritanium, which is needed for the Tier 6 Heavy Duty Plate. There are two planets available, Proteus and Triton, both moons of Neptune. They contain a few new ores, most notably Neutronium, Black Plutonium, Plutonium 241, Uranium 235, Niobium, and Yttrium, which can be harvested either with conventional Ore Drilling Plants or with Void Miners . Deuterium and Ethylene are available as underground fluids from a Fluid Drilling Rig . The Tier 6 Rocket also unlocks the ability to make Planetary Gas Siphons above Neptune for Tritium, Helium-3, Ammonia, and Xenon."
- candidate chunk-10174f2d9d907588e988091242077ef2 doc=doc-45ac5ed5cca700a24048b98b03470923 sections=[Planets] text="The Galaxy Map of the Solar System.\nThere are many different planets, asteroid belts, and star systems within GTNH. To reach them, launch a rocket into space and select a destination on the galaxy map. Some destinations (e.g. moons and space stations) only appear when zoomed into the host planet's orbit. Confirming the location then instantly teleports you to the planet at some very high altitude and inside a lander that carries you down to the surface. The rocket and launch pads are retrieved from the lander upon a complete stop, but sometimes it bugs out and never fully stops. If that happens, place a hopper underneath the lander to retrieve the items. Later on, use the Space Elevator to travel to other planets without any rocket fuel.\nThe tier of a planet (visible in NEI ) determines the minimum tier rocket needed to travel there. For example, a T2 rocket or better is required to reach any T2 planet, such as Mars. There are 8 tiers of rockets in total so the only way to reach the last tier of planets (T9) in the Amun-Ra system is with a Mothership .\nEvery planet has its own set of ore veins, small ore veins, and underground fluid reservoirs (visible in NEI ). Some planets also have unique features such as a specific mob that spawns, a Space Dungeon , or even abandoned structures from past civilizations. The following sections lists all relevant information for every planet with certain items highlighted BLUE to signify high importance or value (e.g. a new ore vein). Note that some planets are gas giants which means a space station must be built in their orbit before using the Planetary Gas Siphon to extract their resources.\nImportant: | See here for a list of dimension IDs. See here for a breakdown of each ore vein. See here for Void Miner drop rates."
- candidate chunk-21a9d8773178e2e4375f6688c2c63c7e doc=doc-8bdfdac1b12c28b48cfd59b3c35795d1 sections=[Highly Ultimate Voltage (UHV) > Progression > Tier 8 Rocket] text="Tier 8 Rocket\nThis is the last regular rocket upgrade. Tier 8 plate ingots need two UHV energy hatches in the assembly line. The rocket unlocks multiple exoplanets. There is no new dungeon, the Shuttle Rocket schematic can be crafted using a schematic from a Pluto dungeon.\n- T Ceti E is required for seaweed, which is used for the new circuit line. A few stacks might be needed to extract the culture.\n- Barnarda C is the only source of Unknown Liquid before UEV, and the trees are needed to manufacture Xenoxene.\n- Vega B has Infinity Catalyst as a small ore, although meteors are an alternative that is available slightly earlier.\n- Barnarda F has Bedrockium as a small ore. While not strictly required, it is a good source for the required large-scale production.\nAwakened Draconium can be found as a small ore on four of the new planets, including Vega B and Barnarda F. Tartarite (used to produce Celestial Tungsten plasma) can be found on Barnarda C and Alpha Centauri Bb."
- evidence chunk-576f35aa4c7d75f033ca70a4306494dd doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets > Space Travel > Building Launchpad] text="Building Launchpad\nThe Rocket requires rocket fuel and a launch pad to launch.\n- A launch pad is formed by placing a 3x3 square on the ground of Galacticraft Launch Pads. Right click to place a rocket on the launch pad.\n- A Galacticraft Fuel Loader can be placed next to the launch pad. It requires 91 EU/s to run. Once a rocket is placed on the launch pad, the Fuel Loader will pipe fuel from its tank into the rocket. A Fuel Loader can be filled with Galacticraft Liquid Canisters, fluid cells or piped in through a tank. All tiers of rocket hold enough fuel capacity for at least one round trip. The Tier 1 Rocket holds up to 5 Buckets of fuel."
- evidence chunk-33ead29e8cd8bacf04be05f96c988afe doc=doc-8666c624815c422fd88b0cdfb5cbfb2a sections=[High Voltage (HV) > Progression > Preparation for making the rocket] text="Preparation for making the rocket\nYou need a NASA workbench and a Implosion Compressor for your first rocket. You will find that the NASA Workbench is a particularly arduous craft that for some reason requires you to use GT tools in a crafting grid in HV. On the other hand, the Implosion Compressor should be trivial for the average player, regardless of setup.\nThe amount of explosives needed for use with the Implosion Compressor is non-trivial, though. You will be expending several stacks of TNT. If your energy setup is based on benzene, you will find that obtaining enough toluene for TNT will be trivial if you upgrade your setup to use a Distillation Tower instead of single-block distilleries. However, if your energy setup is based on oil, you will need some additional processing steps-- diverting some Heavy Fuel for use in a distillery or Distillation Tower will likely be the best way.\nYou'll need 37 T1 heavy-duty alloy plates and a ton of additional materials. Check the quest book. You'll need a T1 rocket, an oxygen equipment set with 2 light oxygen tanks (only 1 is very risky for resource collecting), an oxygen collector and compressor, a fuel loader, and 9 landing pads."
- evidence chunk-9789157d3151f95d6483f88ec29e24bb doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets] text="A Tier 1 Rocket, on launch pad\nThe Rocket is a vehicle added by the Galacticraft mod that enables travel to different planets . These planets often contain ore veins and underground fluid veins which are inaccessible in other dimensions and are required for Tier progression."

Answer status: answer

Answer: To launch a rocket, you need a launch pad formed by placing a 3x3 square of Galacticraft Launch Pads on the ground. Right-click to place a rocket on the launch pad. A Galacticraft Fuel Loader can be placed next to the launch pad; it requires 91 EU/s to run and will pipe fuel from its tank into the rocket once the rocket is placed. The Fuel Loader can be filled with Galacticraft Liquid Canisters, fluid cells, or piped in through a tank. The rocket requires rocket fuel and a launch pad to launch.

Right click to place a rocket on the launch pad.\n- A Galacticraft Fuel Loader can be placed next to the launch pad. It requires 91 EU/s to run. Once a rocket is placed on the launch pad, the Fuel Loader will pipe fuel from its tank into the rocket. A Fuel Loader can be filled with Galacticraft Liquid Canisters, fluid cells or piped in through a tank. All tiers of rocket hold enough fuel capacity for at least one round trip. The Tier 1 Rocket holds up to 5 Buckets of fuel."

- candidate chunk-598e0a37aff2222d5495a1a4ab66d47b doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets > Space Travel > Launch Process] text="Launch Process\nDuring the 20-second ignition period, the rocket produces 100 × T i e r {\\displaystyle {\\displaystyle 100\\times Tier}} pollution/sec and will begin to shake and emit white smoke particles. After the launch begins, the launchpads will break, and the rocket will fly straight up without player steering. While the rocket is flying, it will produce 10000 × T i e r {\\displaystyle {\\displaystyle 10000\\times Tier}} pollution/sec. Note that due to a bug the rocket will actually emit 20x that amount of pollution in version prior to 2.9. A GUI indicator on the left side of the screen will display the rocket's height relative to the world. If the rocket hits any blocks, it will stall and waste fuel, requiring player steering. If a rocket runs out of fuel while launching, it will explode.\nUpon reaching maximum height (y~1100 for the overworld), the player will be transported away from their current dimension and be shown a catalog of solar systems and planets. Clicking on a planet allows the player to build a Space Station there or launch there as the destination on the upper right hand side. Clicking on the planet again will zoom in on it and display its moons and space stations, which can also be launched to. The tier requirement of a launch is listed on the top of the screen; insufficient tier requirements prevent selecting a planet as a destination. The available destinations only depend on the tier of the rocket, not on the launch site.\nUpon selecting a destination, the destination will be loaded in, and the player will descend riding some form of a Lunar Lander (Parachute, Lunar Lander, Landing Balloons, etc.). For the Moon , the player must press space to expend fuel and slow down the Lunar Lander to a descent rate slower than -20m/s to prevent an explosive crash landing. Upon landing, the player can exit the Lander by sneaking. The Lunar Lander will hold the rocket (with its fuel) and launchpads, used for a return flight. Punch to break it like a boat."
- candidate chunk-5ff54a6f190364d05242ad8bcac58ed8 doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets > Crafting Tier 2+ Rockets] text="Crafting Tier 2+ Rockets\nEntrance to a Moon Dungeon\nThe NASA Workbench requires new schematics to craft higher tiers of rockets. These schematics are found in dungeons on planets in tier order. Tier 2 rocket schematics, for example, can be found in the reward room of Moon Dungeons. Dungeon entrances are deep holes on the surface that have unnatural brick blocks on the bottom.\nThe dungeons are naturally generated and so may be difficult to find. It is important to mark the location of each dungeon found as the coordinates are the same across each planet.\nHigher tiers of rockets require different kinds of Rocket Fuels ."
- candidate chunk-dd32ee9a7437c9bdc478d307b750b39f doc=doc-04ef7ad9e1765bcf95cbd804c813863b sections=[Ultimate Voltage (UV) > Progression > Tier 7 Rocket] text="Tier 7 Rocket\nThe Tier 7 Rocket is available as soon as the player is able to power their Assembly Line with two UV Energy Hatches to overclock it to UHV. It unlocks four new planets: Haumea, Makemake, Pluto, and the Kuiper Belt, which should only be mined using Void Miners at this point. The most useful planet to mine is Haumea, which has the earliest source of Nether Star Ore as well as a high chance for Infused Gold Ore, making it very useful for setting up the second tier of Naquadah Fuel. Other new ores are Borax, Red Garnet, Yellow Garnet, and Chrysotile. The available underground fluids are Hydrofluoric Acid on Makemake, and Oxygen, Nitrogen, Fluorine, and Liquid Air on Pluto.\nThe dungeons containing the next tier of schematic are found on Pluto, alongside the Draconic Core Schematic for the Draconic Evolution Fusion Crafter . It is highly recommended to place the Tier 8 Rocket Schematic in a singleblock Scanner (a Research Station in Scanner mode will not work) and duplicate it using a UV Circuit Assembler, because two Tier 8 Rocket Schematics will be needed: one for making the Tier 8 Rocket, and one to be researched for the Shuttle Schematic for the Mothership ."
- candidate chunk-c18096e8b035aa08daa337f0ba995048 doc=doc-8bdfdac1b12c28b48cfd59b3c35795d1 sections=[Highly Ultimate Voltage (UHV) > Progression > Mothership] text="Mothership\nThe Mothership is the end of UHV progression, being required to reach Tier 9 planets and obtain Tengam, the magnetic material in UEV through UMV motors. Creating a Mothership requires launching a Shuttle Rocket while holding the necessary blocks and items, similar to a space station. The Shuttle Rocket's schematic is not found in a dungeon like previous schematics, but is instead crafted in the Assembly Line. Once the Mothership is built, engines must be placed on it and provided with fuel to travel to the Amun-Ra system, which contains the six Tier 9 planets; the only relevant to unlock UEV is Seth, which contains Tengam, as the Space Elevator is a better source of everything else on these planets. A small amount of dilithium from Neper is useful early in UEV. The Space Elevator can be used to travel to them, but only after obtaining MK-III motors, which require the DTPF. The Mothership also requires many previously optional steps in progression, such as the Nano Forge , Radox, and Kevlar."
- candidate chunk-fae1c2141239fe18a731e5541600af29 doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets > Space Travel > Rocket Controls] text="Rocket Controls\n- Interact with the rocket to enter it\n- Interact again or sneak to leave it\n- While on the rocket, press space to ignite the rocket and begin a launch; begins a 20-second countdown to launch, which cannot be cancelled.\n- By default, the key to view the inventory/fuel of the rocket is unbound. It can be found as the \"Open Spaceship Inventory\" key in the Minecraft Controls menu.\n- WASD to steer the rocket while it is flying."
- candidate chunk-bd30c29740c8b96a881cb63d33fa8578 doc=doc-0acf46d9067c834f0c19087002ed00b0 sections=[Insane Voltage (IV) > Progression Goals > Rockets] text="Rockets\nUnlike most tiers, IV contains two different tiers of rockets and associated sets of planets that they unlock. The Tier 3 Rocket is available early in the tier, only being gated behind the IV Assembling Machine to make some of the parts. The Tier 4 Rocket, on the other hand, requires the Assembly Line, meaning it is unavailable until just before the start of LuV. In addition, both tiers require an upgraded rocket fuel, Dense Hydrazine, in order to use, which will require building a Chemical Plant for players who opted to use Cetane-Boosted Diesel for the first two tiers. Most planets from Tier 3 onwards also require an upgraded set of Thermal Padding, which needs PBI to make, and the Tier 4 planet Venus requires a full set of armor with the SpaceSuit property. For a full list of resources available on the Tier 3 and 4 planets, see the Planets page. The Tier 3 rocket also unlocks the ability to make a space station over Jupiter, although the Tier 4 rocket is required to make the Planetary Gas Siphon that makes such a space station useful."

### Lane 2 — memory=true

Answer status: answer

Answer: For the launchpad arrangement, form a 3x3 square on the ground of Galacticraft Launch Pads, then right click to place the rocket on the launch pad. For launch controls: interact with the rocket to enter it, interact again or sneak to leave it, and while on the rocket press space to ignite the rocket and begin a launch. This begins a 20-second countdown to launch, which cannot be cancelled. By default, the key to view the inventory/fuel of the rocket is unbound; it can be found as the "Open Spaceship Inventory" key in the Minecraft Controls menu. WASD steers the rocket while it is flying.
Goal state: "Plan the first Moon trip in GT:NH" (quote "Keep the first Moon trip as my goal.")

Clicking on the planet again will zoom in on it and display its moons and space stations, which can also be launched to. The tier requirement of a launch is listed on the top of the screen; insufficient tier requirements prevent selecting a planet as a destination. The available destinations only depend on the tier of the rocket, not on the launch site.\nUpon selecting a destination, the destination will be loaded in, and the player will descend riding some form of a Lunar Lander (Parachute, Lunar Lander, Landing Balloons, etc.). For the Moon , the player must press space to expend fuel and slow down the Lunar Lander to a descent rate slower than -20m/s to prevent an explosive crash landing. Upon landing, the player can exit the Lander by sneaking. The Lunar Lander will hold the rocket (with its fuel) and launchpads, used for a return flight. Punch to break it like a boat."
- candidate chunk-10174f2d9d907588e988091242077ef2 doc=doc-45ac5ed5cca700a24048b98b03470923 sections=[Planets] text="The Galaxy Map of the Solar System.\nThere are many different planets, asteroid belts, and star systems within GTNH. To reach them, launch a rocket into space and select a destination on the galaxy map. Some destinations (e.g. moons and space stations) only appear when zoomed into the host planet's orbit. Confirming the location then instantly teleports you to the planet at some very high altitude and inside a lander that carries you down to the surface. The rocket and launch pads are retrieved from the lander upon a complete stop, but sometimes it bugs out and never fully stops. If that happens, place a hopper underneath the lander to retrieve the items. Later on, use the Space Elevator to travel to other planets without any rocket fuel.\nThe tier of a planet (visible in NEI ) determines the minimum tier rocket needed to travel there. For example, a T2 rocket or better is required to reach any T2 planet, such as Mars. There are 8 tiers of rockets in total so the only way to reach the last tier of planets (T9) in the Amun-Ra system is with a Mothership .\nEvery planet has its own set of ore veins, small ore veins, and underground fluid reservoirs (visible in NEI ). Some planets also have unique features such as a specific mob that spawns, a Space Dungeon , or even abandoned structures from past civilizations. The following sections lists all relevant information for every planet with certain items highlighted BLUE to signify high importance or value (e.g. a new ore vein). Note that some planets are gas giants which means a space station must be built in their orbit before using the Planetary Gas Siphon to extract their resources.\nImportant: | See here for a list of dimension IDs. See here for a breakdown of each ore vein. See here for Void Miner drop rates."
- candidate chunk-fae1c2141239fe18a731e5541600af29 doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets > Space Travel > Rocket Controls] text="Rocket Controls\n- Interact with the rocket to enter it\n- Interact again or sneak to leave it\n- While on the rocket, press space to ignite the rocket and begin a launch; begins a 20-second countdown to launch, which cannot be cancelled.\n- By default, the key to view the inventory/fuel of the rocket is unbound. It can be found as the \"Open Spaceship Inventory\" key in the Minecraft Controls menu.\n- WASD to steer the rocket while it is flying."
- candidate chunk-c18096e8b035aa08daa337f0ba995048 doc=doc-8bdfdac1b12c28b48cfd59b3c35795d1 sections=[Highly Ultimate Voltage (UHV) > Progression > Mothership] text="Mothership\nThe Mothership is the end of UHV progression, being required to reach Tier 9 planets and obtain Tengam, the magnetic material in UEV through UMV motors. Creating a Mothership requires launching a Shuttle Rocket while holding the necessary blocks and items, similar to a space station. The Shuttle Rocket's schematic is not found in a dungeon like previous schematics, but is instead crafted in the Assembly Line. Once the Mothership is built, engines must be placed on it and provided with fuel to travel to the Amun-Ra system, which contains the six Tier 9 planets; the only relevant to unlock UEV is Seth, which contains Tengam, as the Space Elevator is a better source of everything else on these planets. A small amount of dilithium from Neper is useful early in UEV. The Space Elevator can be used to travel to them, but only after obtaining MK-III motors, which require the DTPF. The Mothership also requires many previously optional steps in progression, such as the Nano Forge , Radox, and Kevlar."
- candidate chunk-576f35aa4c7d75f033ca70a4306494dd doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets > Space Travel > Building Launchpad] text="Building Launchpad\nThe Rocket requires rocket fuel and a launch pad to launch.\n- A launch pad is formed by placing a 3x3 square on the ground of Galacticraft Launch Pads. Right click to place a rocket on the launch pad.\n- A Galacticraft Fuel Loader can be placed next to the launch pad. It requires 91 EU/s to run. Once a rocket is placed on the launch pad, the Fuel Loader will pipe fuel from its tank into the rocket. A Fuel Loader can be filled with Galacticraft Liquid Canisters, fluid cells or piped in through a tank. All tiers of rocket hold enough fuel capacity for at least one round trip. The Tier 1 Rocket holds up to 5 Buckets of fuel."
- candidate chunk-9789157d3151f95d6483f88ec29e24bb doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets] text="A Tier 1 Rocket, on launch pad\nThe Rocket is a vehicle added by the Galacticraft mod that enables travel to different planets . These planets often contain ore veins and underground fluid veins which are inaccessible in other dimensions and are required for Tier progression."
- candidate chunk-dd32ee9a7437c9bdc478d307b750b39f doc=doc-04ef7ad9e1765bcf95cbd804c813863b sections=[Ultimate Voltage (UV) > Progression > Tier 7 Rocket] text="Tier 7 Rocket\nThe Tier 7 Rocket is available as soon as the player is able to power their Assembly Line with two UV Energy Hatches to overclock it to UHV. It unlocks four new planets: Haumea, Makemake, Pluto, and the Kuiper Belt, which should only be mined using Void Miners at this point. The most useful planet to mine is Haumea, which has the earliest source of Nether Star Ore as well as a high chance for Infused Gold Ore, making it very useful for setting up the second tier of Naquadah Fuel. Other new ores are Borax, Red Garnet, Yellow Garnet, and Chrysotile. The available underground fluids are Hydrofluoric Acid on Makemake, and Oxygen, Nitrogen, Fluorine, and Liquid Air on Pluto.\nThe dungeons containing the next tier of schematic are found on Pluto, alongside the Draconic Core Schematic for the Draconic Evolution Fusion Crafter . It is highly recommended to place the Tier 8 Rocket Schematic in a singleblock Scanner (a Research Station in Scanner mode will not work) and duplicate it using a UV Circuit Assembler, because two Tier 8 Rocket Schematics will be needed: one for making the Tier 8 Rocket, and one to be researched for the Shuttle Schematic for the Mothership ."
- candidate chunk-5ff54a6f190364d05242ad8bcac58ed8 doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets > Crafting Tier 2+ Rockets] text="Crafting Tier 2+ Rockets\nEntrance to a Moon Dungeon\nThe NASA Workbench requires new schematics to craft higher tiers of rockets. These schematics are found in dungeons on planets in tier order. Tier 2 rocket schematics, for example, can be found in the reward room of Moon Dungeons. Dungeon entrances are deep holes on the surface that have unnatural brick blocks on the bottom.\nThe dungeons are naturally generated and so may be difficult to find. It is important to mark the location of each dungeon found as the coordinates are the same across each planet.\nHigher tiers of rockets require different kinds of Rocket Fuels ."
- candidate chunk-33ead29e8cd8bacf04be05f96c988afe doc=doc-8666c624815c422fd88b0cdfb5cbfb2a sections=[High Voltage (HV) > Progression > Preparation for making the rocket] text="Preparation for making the rocket\nYou need a NASA workbench and a Implosion Compressor for your first rocket. You will find that the NASA Workbench is a particularly arduous craft that for some reason requires you to use GT tools in a crafting grid in HV. On the other hand, the Implosion Compressor should be trivial for the average player, regardless of setup.\nThe amount of explosives needed for use with the Implosion Compressor is non-trivial, though. You will be expending several stacks of TNT. If your energy setup is based on benzene, you will find that obtaining enough toluene for TNT will be trivial if you upgrade your setup to use a Distillation Tower instead of single-block distilleries. However, if your energy setup is based on oil, you will need some additional processing steps-- diverting some Heavy Fuel for use in a distillery or Distillation Tower will likely be the best way.\nYou'll need 37 T1 heavy-duty alloy plates and a ton of additional materials. Check the quest book. You'll need a T1 rocket, an oxygen equipment set with 2 light oxygen tanks (only 1 is very risky for resource collecting), an oxygen collector and compressor, a fuel loader, and 9 landing pads."
- candidate chunk-6bebc7c1c54fce837efb57613020c769 doc=doc-c9afb2e028c318ddc2f228663f5523fe sections=[Extremely Ultimate Voltage (UEV) > Progression > Tengam] text="Tengam\nThe first step in UEV progression is using the Mothership to travel to Seth , which is the only planet where Raw Tengam Ore can be found. Raw Tengam is then processed in the Magnetic Flux Exhibitor into Purified Tengam, which is the magnetic material for UEV, UIV, and UMV Electric Motors. Once the player obtains their first Purified Tengam, they can make a Tengam Electromagnet, which greatly speeds up the separation process."
- evidence chunk-598e0a37aff2222d5495a1a4ab66d47b doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets > Space Travel > Launch Process] text="Launch Process\nDuring the 20-second ignition period, the rocket produces 100 × T i e r {\\displaystyle {\\displaystyle 100\\times Tier}} pollution/sec and will begin to shake and emit white smoke particles. After the launch begins, the launchpads will break, and the rocket will fly straight up without player steering. While the rocket is flying, it will produce 10000 × T i e r {\\displaystyle {\\displaystyle 10000\\times Tier}} pollution/sec. Note that due to a bug the rocket will actually emit 20x that amount of pollution in version prior to 2.9. A GUI indicator on the left side of the screen will display the rocket's height relative to the world. If the rocket hits any blocks, it will stall and waste fuel, requiring player steering. If a rocket runs out of fuel while launching, it will explode.\nUpon reaching maximum height (y~1100 for the overworld), the player will be transported away from their current dimension and be shown a catalog of solar systems and planets. Clicking on a planet allows the player to build a Space Station there or launch there as the destination on the upper right hand side. Clicking on the planet again will zoom in on it and display its moons and space stations, which can also be launched to. The tier requirement of a launch is listed on the top of the screen; insufficient tier requirements prevent selecting a planet as a destination. The available destinations only depend on the tier of the rocket, not on the launch site.\nUpon selecting a destination, the destination will be loaded in, and the player will descend riding some form of a Lunar Lander (Parachute, Lunar Lander, Landing Balloons, etc.). For the Moon , the player must press space to expend fuel and slow down the Lunar Lander to a descent rate slower than -20m/s to prevent an explosive crash landing. Upon landing, the player can exit the Lander by sneaking. The Lunar Lander will hold the rocket (with its fuel) and launchpads, used for a return flight. Punch to break it like a boat."

- candidate chunk-9789157d3151f95d6483f88ec29e24bb doc=doc-0b20024c57c212406bf68b660f2eaf30 sections=[Rockets] text="A Tier 1 Rocket, on launch pad\nThe Rocket is a vehicle added by the Galacticraft mod that enables travel to different planets . - candidate chunk-14d878ee8526ed79eb222ab4862439f1 doc=doc-8666c624815c422fd88b0cdfb5cbfb2a sections=[High Voltage (HV) > Progression > Blast Off \O/] text="Blast Off \\O/\nYou did it! You crafted your first rocket ever in this modpack. Now you need to fuel it with 5 cells worth of cetane-boosted diesel. Note that rocket will produce a huge amount of pollution if launched. When you land, you should find yourself in a lander. YOU MUST HOLD THE SPACE BAR (or you will die and lose your rocket) to slow down the lander. You should keep your descent velocity to less than 20 m/s to avoid exploding yourself on landing. Try to find the Ilmenite vein (good source of Titanium and Chrome), Bauxite (for Ilmenite and a lot of Aluminium), and Neodymium (for EV+ motors, Fluorine, Phosphorus, and Rare Earth elements that you need later in the game)."
- candidate chunk-c18096e8b035aa08daa337f0ba995048 doc=doc-8bdfdac1b12c28b48cfd59b3c35795d1 sections=[Highly Ultimate Voltage (UHV) > Progression > Mothership] text="Mothership\nThe Mothership is the end of UHV progression, being required to reach Tier 9 planets and obtain Tengam, the magnetic material in UEV through UMV motors. It is important to mark the location of each dungeon found as the coordinates are the same across each planet.\nHigher tiers of rockets require different kinds of Rocket Fuels ."
- candidate chunk-a5f28cb4868a57c9395131f83cfe3cd6 doc=doc-d38e0fb2c49edead8e172a0081f57d61 sections=[Extreme Voltage (EV) > Progression > Your second rocket / Late-EV] text="Your second rocket / Late-EV\nAfter getting the EV field generator you needed for the rocket computer, it's time to build your second rocket. It's a pretty straightforward step. If you forgot or didn't get much meteoric iron, you have to get back to the moon and mine some. I recommend you to have 12 stacks or 45 stacks if you don't want to go back to the moon again for meteoric iron (also for t3 rocket). With the exception of several new parts, which are booster and t2 landing module, the rest goes the same as t1 rocket but is more expensive.\nYou only need a trip to mars for you to get all the resources needed. Also, T2 lander doesn't need you to slow down when approaching as it's a balloon that bounces after it reaches the surface. Your mission there is to collect desh vein (it also has tungsten ore), tungsten vein (also provides lithium), and pitchblende vein (also provides thorium) if you didn't get enough uranium by centrifuging thorium ore. Remember to explore mars more for finding the mars dungeon which contains the T3 rocket blueprint."

For the Moon , the player must press space to expend fuel and slow down the Lunar Lander to a descent rate slower than -20m/s to prevent an explosive crash landing. Upon landing, the player can exit the Lander by sneaking. The Lunar Lander will hold the rocket (with its fuel) and launchpads, used for a return flight. Punch to break it like a boat."
- evidence chunk-dd32ee9a7437c9bdc478d307b750b39f doc=doc-04ef7ad9e1765bcf95cbd804c813863b sections=[Ultimate Voltage (UV) > Progression > Tier 7 Rocket] text="Tier 7 Rocket\nThe Tier 7 Rocket is available as soon as the player is able to power their Assembly Line with two UV Energy Hatches to overclock it to UHV. It unlocks four new planets: Haumea, Makemake, Pluto, and the Kuiper Belt, which should only be mined using Void Miners at this point. The most useful planet to mine is Haumea, which has the earliest source of Nether Star Ore as well as a high chance for Infused Gold Ore, making it very useful for setting up the second tier of Naquadah Fuel. Other new ores are Borax, Red Garnet, Yellow Garnet, and Chrysotile. The available underground fluids are Hydrofluoric Acid on Makemake, and Oxygen, Nitrogen, Fluorine, and Liquid Air on Pluto.\nThe dungeons containing the next tier of schematic are found on Pluto, alongside the Draconic Core Schematic for the Draconic Evolution Fusion Crafter . It is highly recommended to place the Tier 8 Rocket Schematic in a singleblock Scanner (a Research Station in Scanner mode will not work) and duplicate it using a UV Circuit Assembler, because two Tier 8 Rocket Schematics will be needed: one for making the Tier 8 Rocket, and one to be researched for the Shuttle Schematic for the Mothership ."

For the Moon , the player must press space to expend fuel and slow down the Lunar Lander to a descent rate slower than -20m/s to prevent an explosive crash landing. Upon landing, the player can exit the Lander by sneaking. The Lunar Lander will hold the rocket (with its fuel) and launchpads, used for a return flight. Punch to break it like a boat."
